// Teleport Cats (14.2 Cat Paw Addition)
//============================================================
-	script	Teleport Cat#ep14_2	-1,{
	switch(atoi(charat(strnpcinfo(1),16))) {
	case 1:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Entrance to Mora";
		setarray .@cost[0],15;// Amount Canned Food
		break;
	case 2:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Near Eclage","Near Splendide";
		setarray .@cost[0],15,55;
		break;
	case 3:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Entrance to Mora","Midgard Allied Forces Post";
		setarray .@cost[0],55,10;
		break;
	case 4:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Near Splendide","Manuk Field";
		setarray .@cost[0],10,10;
		break;
	case 5:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Midgard Allied Forces Post","Near El Dicastes";
		setarray .@cost[0],10,20;
		break;
	case 6:
		setarray .@map$[0],"Manuk Field";
		setarray .@cost[0],20;
		break;
	}
	mes "[Teleport Cat]";
	mes "What um nice dia, nao e it!";
	mes "Bem-vindo to Cat Mercadou Grupo. We do anything to pou favou nosso customers. We would ate mesmo lie on nosso back se you querer!";
	next;
	mes "[Teleport Cat]";
	mes "We provide teleport service entre various regions based on nosso accumulated knowledge. We aceitar Malangdo Canned Specialties ou Zeny, entao voce pode pagar porem you like it~";
	next;
	set .@menu$,"Forget it:";
	for (.@i = 0; .@i < getarraysize(.@map$); .@i++) {
		set .@menu$, .@menu$+
			.@map$[.@i]+" ("+.@cost[.@i]+" Canned Food):"+
			.@map$[.@i]+" ("+callfunc("F_InsertComma",.@cost[.@i]*1000)+" Zeny):";
	}
	.@i = select(.@menu$) -2;
	if (.@i == -1) {
		mes "[Teleport Cat]";
		mes "Eu vou ver you later entao.";
		close;
	}
	.@choice$ = .@map$[.@i/2];
	.@price = .@cost[.@i/2];
	if (.@i % 2) {
		.@price = .@price*1000;
		if (Zeny < .@price) {
			mes "[Teleport Cat]";
			mes "Eu sou afraid e nao suficiente. Desculpe, mas it nao pode be done fou gratis~";
			close;
		}
		Zeny = Zeny -.@price;
	} else {
		if (countitem(12636) < .@price) {
			mes "[Teleport Cat]";
			mes "Eu sou afraid e nao suficiente. Desculpe, mas it nao pode be done fou gratis~";
			close;
		}
		delitem 12636,.@price;// Malang_Sp_Can
	}
	if (.@choice$ == "Midgard Allied Forces Post") warp "mid_camp",180,247;
	else if (.@choice$ == "Manuk Field") warp "man_fild02",133,47;
	else if (.@choice$ == "Near Splendide") warp "spl_fild02",51,240;
	else if (.@choice$ == "Near El Dicastes") warp "dic_fild01",159,264;
	else if (.@choice$ == "Entrance to Mora") warp "bif_fild02",291,323;
	else if (.@choice$ == "Near Eclage") warp "ecl_fild01",116,309;
	end;
}
ecl_fild01,118,311,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.1	4_M_BOSSCAT
bif_fild02,293,325,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.2	4_M_BOSSCAT
spl_fild02,53,242,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.3	4_M_BOSSCAT
mid_camp,207,234,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.4	4_M_BOSSCAT
man_fild02,135,49,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.5	4_M_BOSSCAT
dic_fild01,161,266,4	duplicate(Teleport Cat#ep14_2)	Teleport Cat No.6	4_M_BOSSCAT

ecl_fild01,97,322,0	script	#ep14_2Entrance	WARPNPC,3,3,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (ep14_2_oliver < 3) {
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Pou favou esperar um minute.";
		mes "Todos primeiro-tempo visitors to Eclage must fill out o necessario forms antes entering.";
		close;
	}
	warp "eclage",100,28;
	end;
}

ecl_fild01,94,322,5	script	Security Guard#ep14_2	4_M_FAIRYSOLDIER,{
	mes "[Security Guard]";
	if (ep14_2_oliver == 0) {
		mes "Todos primeiro-tempo visitors to Eclage must fill out o necessario forms aqui antes entering.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Pou favou underste porque of um recent increase in o influx of pessoas de Midgard atraves Bifrost, we teve que criar este process.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "E claro, o documents sera secured emborum depois o usar in access regulations, entao voce nao tem que preocupar.";
		next;
		if (select("Fill out o forms.:Esquecer it.") == 2) {
			mes "[Security Guard]";
			mes "Pou favou note esse you would nao be capaz de entrar o cidade sem filling out o forms.";
			close;
		}
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Pou favou fill out seu name, occupation, e nivel aqui.";
		next;
		mes "Write seu name.";
		input .@inputstr$;
		next;
		mes "Write seu occupation.";
		input .@inputstr$;
		next;
		mes "Write seu nivel.";
		input .@inputstr$;
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Se you completou seu forms, pou favou submit them to o administratou sobre la.";
		mes "There pode ser alguns waiting tempo, entao pegar seu tempo.";
		ep14_2_oliver = 1;
		setquest 11310;
	} else if (ep14_2_oliver == 1) {
		mes "Pou favou submit seu forms to o administratou sobre lum depois escrevendo in seu name, occupation, e nivel.";
		mes "There pode ser alguns waiting tempo, entao pegar seu tempo.";
	} else if (ep14_2_oliver == 2) {
		mes "Otimo, you completou o forms.";
		mes "Bem-vindo to Eclage, o capital of o Laphines.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Since Eclage is debaixo o influence of Bifrost, hum fluent communication among o races.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "There seria no precisar fou any separate translation, e hum suficiente bridges fou on-pe races, entao I esperancum you have um comfortable visit.";
		ep14_2_oliver = 3;
		completequest 11311;
		close2;
		warp "eclage",100,28;
		end;
	} else {
		mes "Bem-vindo to Eclage, o capital of o Laphines.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "Since Eclage is debaixo o influence of Bifrost, hum fluent communication among o races.";
		next;
		mes "[Security Guard]";
		mes "There seria no precisar fou any separate translation, e hum suficiente bridges fou on-pe races, entao I esperancum you have um comfortable visit.";
	}
	close;
}

// Oliver Wolf Hood (Dreaming Time)

eclage,101,29,0	script	#ep14_2Bridge	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,3,3,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (BaseLevel >= 120) {
		if (ep14_2_oliver < 4) {
			mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
			mes "Hey!!!!!!!";
			mes "Tenhum cuidado sobre la!!";
			emotion ET_HUK, getnpcid(0, "Fairy Carpenter#ep14_2");
			close;
		}
		if (ep14_2_oliver <= 6) {
			mes "- O bridge is quebrado. There deveria ser alguns kind of um solution. -";
			close;
		}
	}
}

		}

	}

		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "O bridge is kind of quebrado entao pou favou tenhum cuidado.";

		mes "Se I were to describe how stupid esse seria... Bem, e like virando um deliciously baked chocolate cake back parum dentro flour.";
		next;

		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "Ahh! Agorum esse I lembrar, antes we usou to be capaz de order o exact necessario length, mas porque they are mass-producing them nowadays, they apenas provide preset lengths.";
		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "To maximize o stability, e better to avoid connecting multiple bridge pieces juntos,";
		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "e este quebrado bridge... de aqui to lum e sobre 19 meters e o prepieces entre 3 meters = 5 meters, e 10 meters......";
		emotion ET_PROFUSELY_SWEAT;
		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "Hmm.......";
		emotion ET_PROFUSELY_SWEAT;
		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "Hmm......";
		mes "......";
		emotion ET_PROFUSELY_SWEAT;
		next;
		mes "[Fairy Carpenter]";
		mes "......Hmm.........";

// Wanted to be Big and Beautiful
//============================================================
function	script	WeightBigQuest	{
	if (!checkweight(1201,1) || (MaxWeight-Weight) < 2000) {
		mes "- Parar Aqui!! -";
		mes "- Voce tem tambem muitos itens. -";
		mes "- You nao pode carregar any mais itens. -";
		mes "- Lighten seu load e -";
		mes "- tentar novamente. -";
		close;
	}
	return;
}

function	script	CheckBurglary	{
	.@last = getarg(1);
	for ( .@i = getarg(0); .@i <= .@last; .@i++ ) {
		if (checkquest(.@i) != -1)
			continue;
		switch(.@i) {
		case 9252:
			mes "- Vamos examine Poppy primeiro -";
			close;
		case 9253:
			mes "- Vamos examine o messy";
			mes "bookshelves primeiro -";
			close;
		case 9254:
			mes "- Vamos examine o damaged";
			mes "book primeiro -";
			close;
		case 9255:
			mes "- Vamos examine the";
			mes "container primeiro -";
			close;
		case 9256:
			mes "- Vamos examine the";
			mes "messed up table primeiro -";
			close;
		}
	}
	return;
}

	end;
}

function	script	PranksterDiag	{
	if (checkquest(4265) <= 1) {
		mes "- I saw um book e stretched meu he out to reach fou it. Eu acho e sobre Midgard. -";
		next;
		mes "[Dominic]";
		mes "Hey, outsider who disse voce pode touch meu books?";
		mes "Ficar emborum de meu bookshelves!";
		close;
	}
	return;
}

ecl_tdun04,37,21,0	duplicate(#pf)	#pf3	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,2,2

function	script	Wallnotes	{
	next;
	if (getarg(1,0) == 0) {
		if (select("Nao read it!:Read it.") == 1) {
			mes "It nao parecer muito interesting at todos!";
			close;
		}
		mes "Read atraves o notes.";
		next;
	}
	else if (select("Parar lendo.:Read it novamente.") == 1) {
		mes "E nao like it needs to be memorized entao Eu vou apenas parar lendo.";
		close;
	}
	mes "[Wall Note "+ getarg(0) +"]";
	switch( getarg(0) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "I succeeded in putting Bifrost to sleep.";
		mes "Eu sou apenas entao incrivel!";
		mes "Mas pou que Eu tenho to remain in um lugar like this, tambem?";
		return;
	case 2:
		mes "It nao tem been tambem long desde Eu estavum imprisoned in o torre, mas meu memorias are leaving me entao rapidly.";
		mes "It pode ser Bifrost's forte will to ficar awake esse is affecting me este caminho.";
		next;
		mes "[Wall Note 2]";
		mes "I imaginar se Eu vou esquecer ate mesmo meu name... How it worries me...";
		return;
	case 3:
		mes "Meu appearance is mudando!";
		mes "Meu hands, meu skin...";
		mes "Este is sem um doubt o form of um worm!";
		mes "Eu nao consigo acreditar Eu sou mudando parum dentro um worm..";
		mes "How could este be!";
		next;
		mes "[Wall Note 3]";
		mes "Isentao e tambem porque of Bifrost?";
		mes "Is it Bifrost's curse against me fou putting a to sleep?";
		mes "Is it fazendo este to me while e asleep?";
		mes "Eu nao consigo acreditar it... Meu goodness!";
		return;
	case 4:
		mes "Bifrost's cabecum is aindum crescendo continuously. Eu nao consigo acreditar this... Elum e manter stretching beyond o torre!";
		mes "Could it be a forte will, is aindum awake e is tentando to send um message?";
		return;
	case 5:
		mes "O pessoum who sera debaixo atacar depois Bifrost is none outro que me!";
		mes "I feel um eerie gaze directed at me!";
		mes "Eu nao consigo apenas sit aqui e pegar it.";
		next;
		mes "[Wall Note 5]";
		mes "Monstros, Eu tenho to criar mais monstros to proteger me. As muitos as I could possibly criar!";
		return;
	}
	return;
}

function	script	EclageinEntrance	{
	if ((isbegin_quest(7411) + isbegin_quest(7412) + isbegin_quest(7413)) == 3) {
		hideoffnpc "Eclage Guard#tl01";
		cutin "minuel01",4;
		mes "[Eclage Guard]";
		mes "Com licencum fou um moment.";
		mes "Is lum anyone de Splendide aqui?";

	monster "que_avan01",29,32,"DARK_PRIEST",1198,1,"#avtmanager::OnMyMobDead";
	end;

	hideonnpc "Avant's Summon#tlact04";

	hideonnpc "Hisie#tlhunt";
	hideoffnpc "#tltimer";
	$111204_avantlabo = 0;
	end;

		mapannounce "que_avan01","Hisie: This is it for you! Avant!!",bc_map,"0x7b68ee";

		hideoffnpc "Hisie#tlhunt";

	end;
